Detail in ADR-004 section 5a after "1b.9d".

### S1b 2026-11-21 work package 1b.9e (`Resume` of the production build: `state.json`, six stages, the verification of what a resume trusts, the lock, in `SuffixTree.Esa` (`Sesa/SesaResumeState.cs`, `Sesa/SesaResume.cs`, `Sesa/SesaBuildLock.cs`, `SesaBuilder`, `SaBuilder`, `MappedOutputFile.OpenExisting`), tests and the Bench command `sesa resume`; owner rule "no debts before S2"; one implementer context with one compaction, one container restart at the start with nothing lost)

- 2026-11-21 1b.9e [x] `57f6b50b` (tests first `f6177750`, the build `7340848f`, the Bench command and the drivers `a96e3785`, the cover made once and the empty text `ec5464a2`, tests for the survivors `7c7e7ce8`, `9a956ec9`, `427b7f33`, the mutant list `5c8fb3a6`, the audit `e4034910`, `867492b2`, the measurements `57f6b50b`, the ADR record `886f5e82`, `analysis/em/*1b9e*`, `analysis/mutation/*1b9e*`; this entry in the commit after them). **Outcome in one paragraph:** (1) **`SesaBuildOptions.Resume` is implemented and the gate is met.** A build with `Resume = true` takes the lock `<target>.lock`, records its input fingerprint (n, alphabet, counts, SHA-256) and, after each of six stages (PH0 `packed`, PH1 `ranks`, PH2-PH3 `sa`, PH4-PH5 `lcp`, the BWT file, PSI), the outputs it wrote (their CRC-32C, forced to storage) in `<target>.tmp.d/state.json` (a JSON object whose last member is its own CRC, replaced atomically); a later call with `Resume = true` verifies the state (the key, then every recorded range and file by CRC) and skips the stages that verify; a failed build keeps its temporary file and state; the default (`Resume = false`) is the build as it was (no state, no lock, no extra fsync; 1,095 External tests that pin the counters unchanged and green). The parameters that cannot change a byte (threads, X, K, F, windows, seed, scale) are **not** in the key; the rank file's cover (X) is the one intermediate that is. (2) **Kills:** **259 `kill -9` landed in 264 cases (223 at 10^7 over DNA, a repetitive text and a protein-like text; 36 at 10^8 over random and repetitive DNA; 93 more kills in 96 cases of a second series at 10^7 gave the same result): the container was byte-identical to the uninterrupted build and nothing but the target was left in all 264**, at every one of 20 boundaries and at random moments inside every phase, with the resume itself killed again in a quarter of the cases and resumed at 1, 2 or 4 threads at random; a kill during the publication left either no target or a complete one (26 complete, never damaged); two processes: the second exits 3 with the lock message; RssAnon of the finishing runs at most 252 MB. (3) **What a resume saves** (resume against the restart of the build before the package, `dna_100m`): nothing resolved before the SA is done (PH0 and PH1 are under 1.2 s at 10^8), 34 % after it (6.5 s), 77 % after PH4-PH5 (13.7 s), 88 % after the BWT, 94 % in PH7 (17.2 s of 18.2 s); the expected loss of a kill at a random moment 2.6 s (15 %) against 8.7 s (50 %). **PH2-PH3 and PH4-PH5 are not resumable inside**: the worst case of a kill is the phase it is in (5.5 s and 7.3 s of 17.4 s on `dna_100m`; 14.4 s of 25.9 s in PH2-PH3 of the repetitive `rep_100m`), a leaf journal of PH3 would be exact and is sketched (ADR-004 section 5a "1b.9e"); it would save up to 43 % of a repetitive build killed late in PH3 and is proposed as a follow-up package, the owner decides. (4) **Cost:** recording is **0.31 s of CRC passes + 25 ms of state files + 2 ms of fsyncs at 10^8 (1.9 % of the build), 0.04 s at 10^7 (1.9 %)**; the device writes are the same to the megabyte; the in-process A/B does not resolve it from the noise (Resume on against off 0.95-1.06 at the medians, 0.89-1.15 pair by pair); **Resume off against the build before the package: 0.96-1.02 at the medians, no measurable slowdown**. (5) **Tests:** **306 new tests** (19 of the format, the lock and the reopening of the container, 281 of the build, 6 of PH1-PH3 with a checkpoint of their own); the default set of `SuffixTree.Esa.Tests` 3,989 + 10 skipped -> **4,286 passed + 19 skipped**; red before green: at the first commit 182 of the 204 cases of the build were red, 13 guards green, 9 skipped; the first full run of the class had 12 failures, all mistakes of the tests, none of the code; the tests of texts of 0-2 symbols found one real defect of the verification (below). **Mutation audit: 235 mutants, 177 + 2 killed, 42 closed by a test, 14 argued equivalent, 0 open.** (6) **Directory `fsync` is not possible in the BCL** (`File.OpenHandle` of a directory throws `UnauthorizedAccessException`, tried): a question for the owner, the consequence argued (never a wrong container, a lost final rename leaves a complete `.tmp` that the next `Resume` call publishes). ADR-004 section 5a "1b.9e" has the tables.
- **Defects found.** In the build, outside the scope (reported, not fixed): two builds of one target without `Resume` destroy each other (the second's `DeleteStale` unlinks the first's temporary file, the first's rename then publishes the second's half-written file); `DifferenceCover.Create` costs 0.27 s per build at X = 993 (11 % of a build of 10^7 symbols); a plain build after a killed resumable one leaves the empty lock file; the existing test `NoAllocationProportionalToTheTextForTheTotal_AndOnlyTheResultForTheLengths` is flaky under load in Release. In the package, fixed: the verification refused `NODE_COUNT` of the empty text (found by the tests of texts of 0-2 symbols); the first version made the difference cover twice and was 12-17 % slower than the build without Resume at 10^7 (found by the first A/B); two mistakes of my kill and timing drivers (the variation of threads in a timing, the lock file of a killed build counted as a leftover of the restart), corrected before the numbers above. In the process: four commits (`5c8fb3a6`, `427b7f33`, `e4034910`, `867492b2`) were pushed without the two trailer lines (my omission, not rewritten because they were already published).
- **Not verified / limits.** A kill inside PH2-PH3 or PH4-PH5 loses that phase; the power loss and the durability of directory operations (no device, no `fsync` of a directory in the BCL); Linux and ext4 only (macOS, Windows, arm64, network and FUSE volumes not run); a state edited by hand and sealed again is a forgery (the checks of the layout refuse what they can, the depth and rank of the longest repeat and MAX_LCP rest on the CRC); a kill between the rename and the removal of the scratch makes the next call rebuild; a resumed build trusts the snapshot of the input that PH0 scanned; `Fsync = false` with `Resume` is safe against `kill -9` only; 3x10^9 symbols were not built (the figures there are estimates by size); a protein-like text of 10^8 was not built; single-site hand-made mutants by the implementer.
- **Open points handed to the owner.** (1) Directory `fsync`: one P/Invoke (a `libc` `fsync` of a directory descriptor) or the documented consequence. (2) Should `Resume` be the default of the production entry point (the numbers above; a failed build keeps 13 bytes per character on the disk and takes a lock: behaviour changes). (3) The leaf journal of PH3 (about 40 % of a repetitive build killed late in PH3) as a follow-up package. (4) A lock for builds without `Resume`, or the statement that two builds of one target are unsupported. (5) A static cache of the difference cover (0.27 s per build).

### S1b 2026-11-21 work package 1b.9e.1 (a resumed build trusts only what a kept stage vouches for: every other byte of the temporary container is made zero, in `SuffixTree.Esa` (`MappedOutputFile` `ZeroFill.ClearOutside` and `OpenExisting`, `Sesa/SesaResume.cs`, one call in `SesaBuilder`), tests, the Bench rows and `analysis/em`; correction of 1b.9e after the orchestrator's verification; one implementer context, no compaction, no restart)

- 2026-11-21 1b.9e.1 [x] `fc617cfc` (tests first `df637c3c`, the fix `8c6a338b`, the measurements `11c37f89`, tests for the survivors `bd73aced`, the audit `fc617cfc`, `analysis/em/*1b9e1*`, `analysis/mutation/*1b9e1*`; this entry in the commit after them). **Outcome in one paragraph:** (1) **The finding was real and is fixed.** A resumed build trusted every byte of the temporary container that lies outside a kept, CRC-verified range; reproduced on the orchestrator's 10^7 DNA with my own driver (`run_1b9e1_damage.py repro`): one flipped bit of the last byte of the recorded CHILD range gives **9** differing bytes after the resume, `0xA5` from the end of the last recorded range at `packed` / `sa` **10,971 / 8,507**, and the whole complement of the kept ranges filled **11,047** at `lcp-child-built`, `psi-built` and `info-written`; after the fix **0 in all**. (2) **The fix:** at every resume, after the state is verified and before any stage runs, every byte of the container outside the ranges of the kept stages is made zero: `SesaResumeRun.KeptContainerRanges()` hands the ranges, `MappedOutputFile.OpenExisting(path, truncateTo, keep)` cuts as before and calls `ZeroFill.ClearOutside`, which reads the rest in chunks of 1 MiB, compares with zero and writes zeros, **in place and before the file is mapped**, over exactly the 4,096-byte units that hold a non-zero byte; nothing is allocated or freed (the reservation of 1b.7b and 1b.9d stays), a clean file is only read, the header page is part of it (PH7 writes it whole, magic last), the state is not touched, a kill in the middle is harmless (idempotent, re-derived from the state). The report carries what was read, written and how long it took. `Resume = false` is untouched. Alternatives weighed and measured: zeros over everything (the same time, 1.0-2.0 GB more at the device for nothing), `ftruncate` down and up (free, but sparse: the reservation is lost), only the padding and the header (does not cover CHILD), verify and refuse (the same read, loses the work). (3) **Tests:** the behavioural tests committed first, **365 of 397 red** against `ff71de38`, green after the fix; 459 cases in process (garbage with two patterns outside the kept ranges at every boundary on four kinds of LCP tail, with and without PSI, inside the phases, one bad byte in every gap and in the header page, a damaged CHILD slot, a resume that dies again) and 81 unit cases of the normalisation; **717 resumes in new processes** (18 markers x 11 kinds of damage at 10^6, 10^7 and 10^8), all exit 0, byte-identical, nothing left. (4) **The campaign with the new code:** **53 `kill -9` landed in 64 cases (39 at 10^7 over DNA, a repetitive and a protein-like text, 14 at 10^8), all byte-identical, nothing left**, RssAnon of the finishing runs at most 255.8 MB. (5) **Cost** (what a resume saves, the restart, the resume before and the resume of this package from one killed state, 300 runs): the normalisation reads 0.02-1,300 MB and takes **0.01-0.28 s at 10^8 (median 0.18 s) and 0.01-0.045 s at 10^7**, 1.5 % of a restart at most, and writes only what is wrong (0 for a kill at a boundary, 229 / 324 MB after a kill in the middle of PH3 / PH4-PH5 at 10^8, +330 MB at the device and +1.4 s in the one cell where it is visible); the resume still saves 7.4-8.4 s of 19.5-20.6 s once the SA is kept and 16.6-18.4 s after PH4-PH5; the worst case (every byte outside the kept ranges damaged) costs +1.0-2.0 GB at the device and +1.1-2.6 s at 10^8. **`Resume = false` in process against `ff71de38`: 0.91-1.01 at 10^7, 0.97-0.99 at 10^8: no measurable slowdown.** (6) **Mutation audit: 61 mutants, 51 + 3 killed, 3 closed by a test, 4 argued equivalent, 0 open.** ADR-004 section 5a "1b.9e.1" has the tables.
- **Defects found.** In 1b.9e (fixed here): a resumed build trusted every byte outside a kept range; its tests could not tell a kill from damage (the one test that poisoned CHILD used the final values of half of its slots). By reading, not changed: the build does not run `SesaVerifier` on what it publishes; its `Sections` and `Structure` levels would have refused the padding and the CHILD slots of the damaged containers (not run). In my process: a driver parsed the Bench rows without their first column (found by the first run, fixed before any figure was kept); a scripted edit deleted a class (the build failed at once, restored from git).
- **Not verified / limits.** The cost is linear in the part of the container that is not kept and measured at 10^7 and 10^8 on a warm page cache; 3x10^9 (39 GB to read before the SA is complete) is an estimate by size (8 s warm); a power loss in the middle of the clearing is argued, not tested; a page of a kept range changed by another process between the CRC pass and its use is not detected; Linux and ext4 only; single-site hand-made mutants by the implementer.
- **Open points handed to the owner.** (1) An OS call (`fallocate` with `ZERO_RANGE`) would make the clearing of a large unkept part a metadata operation: not needed at these sizes and not written (managed code only); it could ride on the decision about the directory `fsync` of 1b.9e. (2) Should the build run the `Sections` level of the verifier (every byte outside the payloads is zero) before the rename (0.3 s at 10^8)? (3) Points 2-5 of 1b.9e stand.

### S1b 2026-11-22 work package 1b.9g (the readers of a tree over a container without a fence: an asymmetric protocol with thread-exclusive cells and a process-wide barrier in `Dispose`, `ReaderCell.cs` and `ReaderGate.cs` in `SuffixTree.Esa`; the mapped descent without `AggressiveOptimization`; 42 new tests; the in-process A/B client `abapp` in `analysis/bench/abapp`; no csproj, no `apps/`, no builder, no container format, no public API; one implementer context, the container restarted once)

- 2026-11-22 1b.9g [x] `fdad7ee1` (tests first `8c9226ca`; the protocol `0cb5c9f4`; the inlined `Enter` and the scope that carries the gate `374054cc`; the mapped descent `de87b762`; the instruments `ebc02348`; the scope of an in-memory call `25d50ce3`; the audit, the rows and the tables `fdad7ee1`; this entry in the commit after them). **Outcome in one paragraph:** (1) **Item 1 is implemented and proven; the gate is met on the in-process instrument and not met on one series of `ReadBench mt:`.** The calls of a tree over a container are a plain store of the tree's 64-bit id into the cell of the thread, a read of the flag and a plain store that clears it (5.5 ns per call at one and at four threads against 12.8-13.1 ns for the striped count of 1b.8; the registration 4.5 ns against 11.9); `Dispose` runs one process-wide barrier and one scan and does not wait. The proof (x64 TSO and arm64, the order of the flag store, the barrier and the count reads, what the barrier does not give, the registry, the contract of `Dispose`) is in ADR-004 section 5a "1b.9g". At four threads, the facade over the index behind it (`abapp`, null experiment first, balanced for the bias of the second spec): **0.975-1.002 on all twelve figures on the median (the head 0.814-0.965), cleared by the spread on 9 of 12**; **`ReadBench mt:` on DNA: 0.961 / 0.987 / 0.953 pooled (the head 0.991 / 0.983 / 0.982), spread 0.85-1.15 between processes: not met**, the cause isolated to the cache line of the gate's flags (a 27-line patch, 0.982 / 1.002 / 0.982, **not adopted**: 80 % of the time cap had passed; `analysis/em/1b9g_gateflags_candidate.patch`) [superseded by 1b.9g.1: applied]. The abandon rule did not fire [superseded by 1b.9g.1: the orchestrator reads it as both instruments] (read as the row's in-process instrument; if it is read as both instruments it is triggered on `ReadBench` DNA until the patch is applied: the owner's reading). (2) **The cache-resident cost of the mapped path was `[MethodImpl(AggressiveOptimization)]` on `MappedSearch.Run`**, not the list (materialisation 104.8 ns in memory and mapped) and not the decode; removed (one line, a pin test): mapped / in-memory, one thread, count / locate / absent: DNA 0.987 / 0.865 / 0.961 -> **0.998 / 1.073 / 0.992**, the orchestrator's protein set 0.804 / 0.704 / 0.955 -> **0.943 / 1.023 / 0.971**; the large sets of 1b.9b move by at most 2.2 points; the price is about 50-70 ms once per process over the first 60,000-100,000 queries (a fresh process). What is left: the 20-symbol count 6 % behind the in-memory tree (the packed-symbol decode of the child scan). (3) **42 new tests** (36 against stubs: 34 red; one red on the first version of `Enter`; the pin test red with the attribute; the audit's two last red on their mutants), the race client over 410 processes (80 on the head, 330 on the change: all clean; two mutants of the protocol crash 40 of 40), **73 hand-made mutants: 68 killed, 5 argued, none open**. Production code: 4 files, +369/-49.

Mine, not fixable in a pushed commit: the message of `de87b762` gives 7 ns for the checks of the mapped descent, the rows give 13 of 79 ns. In the instruments: the second-loaded spec of `abapp` is favoured by 2-7 % (the verifications of 1b.9a and 1b.9b measured with the index first, so their figures for the reader count were slightly too good); `ReadBench mt:` cannot resolve 3 % at four threads (spread 0.85-1.15; the head measured 0.924 on DNA count in 1b.9b and 0.991 here); the orchestrator's "uniform protein" patterns are 12 letters of a DNA text used on a protein text (calls of 0.3 microseconds, six times shorter than the present 12-mers of the same text); the variant `ix-raw` of `ReadBench` returns 0 counts on a container with the default tagged child table (`ix-rawtagged` works). `FacadeReaderCountTests` described a count that no longer exists (its summary is rewritten).

the cause is read in `EsaSuffixIndex.cs` (`result = default;` at the top of `TryExtendLeft`, line 109, and of `Extend`, line 149, before `locus` is read). **The README samples** (six blocks): I ran the in-memory blocks on the head and the container blocks against containers built by the Bench `sesa build` (a 300,007-symbol DNA text with 23 `GATTACA`, cross-checked by a plain count in Python, and a 1,350-byte UTF-8 text with 150 `café`): every printed value equals the comment (`[0, 7]`, `abra`, count 2 and 2 positions, six children and `a` first, the node of `bra` at depth 3, MEMs of length 7 and 4, `cadabra`, 54 distinct substrings, the four ways of opening give 23, the UTF-8 needle 150 / the one-char needle 0 / a code unit above 255 0, `ObjectDisposedException` after `Dispose`). **The limits table** (21 items): items 5, 6 and 10 checked against the code and ADR-003 (`MaxTextLength = int.MaxValue - 1` for the facade, `symbols >= int.MaxValue - 1` refused by the `Structure` pass, `letterWords[i] > byte.MaxValue` refused by `SesaHeaderReader`, the ADR-003 amendment of 2026-11-18); the others are the reviewer's measurements and the ADR paragraphs they cite, not re-measured by me. **`PLAN.md`:** the diff is the Status block alone (14 added lines); stages, projection, gates and estimates are unchanged. **The "2.7x" claim** of ADR-004 A1: the gate row 1.138 s over 0.3442 s is 3.31, 2.7 is the median of the 36 cold builds: the reviewer is right, corrected in the ADR with the reason. **The verdicts on the 1b.11 cells:** C14 = 2 and C15 = 1 accepted (the same builder stands under F03 = 1; my own doubt about C14 was on record), C10 = 3 kept and flagged `U` with the base stated; the matrix, the README headline (2.20, rank 4, first place in 0.4 % of the weightings) and the `PLAN.md` Status are regenerated or corrected (`analysis/review/1b11_rescore.md` section 9; five line pairs differ, all in the Seqeron column and the ranking line). **Other corrections made by me:** the status lines of ADR-001, -002 and -003 ("proposed" -> implemented and measured in S1a / S1b), the framework table of `Suffix_Tree.md` (`.NET 8` / `.NET 9` -> `.NET 10`, the three projects checked with `dotnet msbuild -getProperty:TargetFramework`), the status paragraph of the directory README. **Not re-run by me:** the reviewer's long sets (default 4,945 + 37 skipped, Exhaustive 82, Fuzz 39, Vectors 632, checked build 4,964 + 18 skipped, the harness with seed 424242: 112,197 cases and 0 failures, racebench 40 of 40, three chains at 10^6 and 10^7: 11 PASS each); their counts equal mine from 1b.9h / 1b.9 on the same production code. **Triage of the 16 findings** (the debt register H below has the full list): **R01 is a defect in a public member and a debt before S2: work package 1b.12**; R05 and R07 closed by me; R02, R03, R04 and the Minor / Nit / Info items are decisions of the owner, S2 or S8 and go into the S2 message. **Defects found by the verification:** none beyond the review's. **Cost of this verification:** about 25 minutes (the start time was not logged: an estimate; the machine clock read 21:47 UTC when the records were written; the reproducer and the samples took about 10 minutes, the corrections and the records about 15); tokens an estimate of 0.15 M.

### S1b 2026-11-23 work package 1b.12 (the fix of finding R01 of the review of 1b.10: the result of a navigation call may be the same variable as the locus; `src/` +23/-14 in 5 files, 12 lines of code in `EsaSuffixIndex.cs` and the rest comments and the README (`EsaSuffixIndex.cs`, `EsaSuffixIndex.Tree.cs`, the README of `SuffixTree.Esa`, and doc comments in two files of `SuffixTree.Core`), no hot loop, no container format, no public signature, no ADR text; 106 new tests in four files of `SuffixTree.Esa.Tests`; one implementer context)

- 2026-11-23 1b.12 [x] `08c904cd` (the fix; tests first `6af59503`; the mutation audit `cf07f0b9`; the records in the commits after them). **Outcome in one paragraph:** `tree.TryExtendRight(l, c, out l)` (both overloads) and `tree.TryExtendLeft(l, c, out l)` give the answer that two variables give. At the unfixed head **57 of the 106 new tests fail** (the span and the symbol overload of `TryExtendRight`, `TryExtendLeft`, the review's `TheResultMayAliasTheLocus`, the natural loop, the random walks); at `08c904cd` all 106 pass. **The review's claim about the other members held and is now proved by tests that pass on the unfixed head:** `TryParent`, `TryChild`, `TrySuffixLink`, `GetChildren` (also with the children written into the array that holds the node, `GetChildren(kids[1], kids)`) and `Locate` (its positions written over the memory of the locus) are not affected. The fix is `long length = locus.Length;` moved in front of `result = default;` in `Extend`, and `IndexLocus at = locus;` (one 32-byte copy) at the top of `TryExtendLeft`; the symbol overload of `TryExtendRight` needed no change of its own. 46 own mutants of the change and of the members around it: **44 killed, 2 survive and are equivalent**; no test gap.
- **What changed.** `EsaSuffixIndex.cs` (`Extend`: the read of the depth before the first write to `result`; `TryExtendLeft`: the copy `at` for every later read of the locus, so that the empty-string branch calls `TryExtendRight` with a variable that is never the result), `EsaSuffixIndex.Tree.cs` (a comment at `TryParent`, `TryChild`, `TrySuffixLink`, `GetChildren`: they resolve the locus into a handle before they write; no code), `ISuffixIndex.cs` and `ISuffixTreeNavigation.cs` in `SuffixTree.Core` (a `<remarks>` on each member that has an out locus or a destination of loci: "may be the same variable as the locus: the answer is the same as with two variables"; comments only), the README of `SuffixTree.Esa` (limit 10: "do not pass one variable ..." became the guarantee, numbering kept, with the test that pins it; the second sample advances one variable: `if (!tree.TryExtendRight(locus, c, out locus)) { ... break; }`; the sample was compiled and run again, it prints the same: locus `[2..3] depth 3 aux 4`, count 2, positions 7 and 0, 6 children of the root, the edge symbol `a`, `abra` explicit, the link `[6..7] depth 3` with count 2). The result is still assigned on every path (`result = default` after the reads on the failure paths). The container format, every hot loop (`Count`, `Locate`, the descent of `EsaIndex.Search`) and every public signature are untouched; the 1b.9 gate rows were therefore not measured again, and `abapp` was not run (no line of the count / locate path changed).
- **Tests first, red then green (the exact account).** The tests were committed first (`6af59503`, no change of `src/`) and run at that head, whose `src/` is that of `12495c3e`: `dotnet test tests/SuffixTree/SuffixTree.Esa.Tests -c Release --no-build --filter "FullyQualifiedName~Aliasing"`: **Failed 57, Passed 49, Total 106**; the per-fixture outcome and the first line of every failure message is `analysis/mutation/1b12_red_run.tsv`. **Red (by test, fixtures in brackets):** `TheResultMayAliasTheLocus` (facade, 3 layouts, 3 of 3: "aliased advance: false at 'b'", the review's text), `TheResultMayAliasTheLocus_AdvancingOneVariableAlongAString` (7 of 7), `TheNaturalLoop_OneVariableAdvancedAlongEverySubstring_ReachesTheLocusOfTheOracle` (7 of 7), `TryExtendRight_Symbol_TheResultMayAliasTheLocus` (7 of 7), `TryExtendRight_Span_TheResultMayAliasTheLocus` (7 of 7), `TryExtendLeft_TheResultMayAliasTheLocus` (7 of 7, `ArgumentException`), the facade's `TryExtendRight_SymbolOverload_...`, `TryExtendRight_SpanOverload_...`, `TryExtendLeft_...` and `EveryLocusOfAText_ThroughTheFacade_...` (3 of 3 each), and `RandomWalks_TheAliasedCallAndTheSeparateCallGiveTheSameAnswer_AndTheAnswerOfTheOracle` (7 of 8 engines; the Ukkonen adapter of the oracle passes). **Green at the unfixed head (49):** `TryParent_...`, `TryChild_...`, `TrySuffixLink_...`, `GetChildren_TheChildrenMayOverwriteTheVariableThatHoldsTheNode`, `GetChildren_TheChildrenOfTheSecondChild_MayBeWrittenOverTheArrayThatHoldsIt`, `Locate_ThePositionsMayBeWrittenOverTheBytesOfTheLocus` (7 of 7 each), the facade's `TryParent_TryChild_TrySuffixLink_...` and `GetChildren_ChildrenWrittenOverTheArrayThatHoldsTheNode` (3 of 3 each) and the walks of the Ukkonen adapter. After the fix: the same command, **106 passed** (about one second; `dotnet test ... --filter "FullyQualifiedName~Aliasing"` at `08c904cd`).
- **Which members were affected (every member that has an `in IndexLocus` and an `out IndexLocus`, or a span that may hold the locus, found by reading `ISuffixIndex`, `ISuffixTreeNavigation`, `ISuffixArrayAccess` and the facade).**

| member | affected at `12495c3e` | how it was proved |
|---|---|---|
| `TryExtendRight(in locus, int, out result)` | **yes**: `false` for a string that occurs (`abr` + `b`), and **`true` for one that does not** (from `"a"`, `'a'` gives the locus of `"a"` again: `Extend` compared the symbol with the first symbol of the interval because the cleared depth was 0); the next call throws `ArgumentException` | red at the unfixed head in 7 of 7 engines (`TryExtendRight_Symbol_...`) and 3 of 3 facades; green after; mutants A01-A11, D01-D03, E01, E02 killed |
| `TryExtendRight(in locus, ReadOnlySpan<char>, out result)` | **yes**: a locus of the wrong depth (`abr` + `a`: depth 1, expected 4), and an empty pattern returns depth 0 instead of "the locus itself" | red in 7 of 7 and 3 of 3; green after; the same mutants |
| `TryExtendLeft(in locus, int, out result)` | **yes**: `ArgumentException` for every locus of a non-empty text (the root included, by reading: the cleared locus fails `CheckLocus`) | red in 7 of 7 and 3 of 3; green after; A12-A20 killed |
| `TryParent(in locus, out parent)` | no: `Resolve` copies the locus into the handle `EsaNode` before `parent = default` | green at the unfixed head (7 of 7, 3 of 3, every locus of three texts, every argument, both forms, the oracle); mutants B01-B03, C04 (the write put before the read, a re-read of the locus after the write) are killed, so the test can see the defect there |
| `TryChild(in node, int, out child)` | no (same reason; a non-node is refused by both forms) | green at the unfixed head; B04, B05, C05 killed |
| `TrySuffixLink(in node, out link)` | no (same reason) | green at the unfixed head; B06, B07, C06 killed |
| `GetChildren(in node, Span<IndexLocus>)` | no: the handle is copied before the first child is written; the destination may hold the variable of the node (every slot, sizes from too small to larger than the number of children) | green at the unfixed head (7 of 7, 3 of 3, `GetChildren(kids[1], kids)`, a too small array writes nothing and keeps the node); B08, B09, B10, C07 killed |
| `Locate(in locus, long, Span<long>)` | no: the arguments are read before `EsaIndex.Locate` writes (the destination is cast over the memory of the locus) | green at the unfixed head (7 of 7); L01, L02 killed |
| `Count`, `IsExplicitNode`, `EdgeSymbol` (a locus in, no locus or span out), `Lca`, `SuffixAt`, `RankOf`, `LcpAt`, `Lcp` (no locus in) | not applicable | by reading the interfaces |

  Not tested, by design: a **pattern span that overlaps the result** (`TryExtendRight(l, chars, out r)` with `chars` cast over the memory of `r`): an input span over the memory of an output is undefined in .NET unless a member says otherwise, and `Extend` clears `result` before it reads the pattern; no caller writes that. Reported, not changed.
- **The tests** (`tests/SuffixTree/SuffixTree.Esa.Tests`, default set, deterministic, about one second for the 106): `Contract/IndexAliasingTests.cs` (per member and overload, for every locus of `abracadabra`, `mississippi` and `aaaaaaa` (the root, the points inside edges, the nodes, the leaves with and without the terminator, the leaf of the empty suffix) and every argument including the symbols that are no code unit, aliased call against the two-variable call against the definitional oracle `IndexContractOracle`; a non-node refused by both forms; `GetChildren` with the node in every slot; `Locate` over the locus bytes; parameterised over the seven engines that have the navigation members: `esa`, `esa-packed`, `esa-reference3`, `esa-facade`, `esa-facade-packed`, `esa-file`, `esa-file-trusted`), `Facade/FacadeAliasingTests.cs` (the concrete `EsaSuffixTree`, three layouts: the review's `TheResultMayAliasTheLocus`, one test per member and overload, every locus of `mississippi`), `Contract/IndexAliasingPropertyTests.cs` (**the property test**: 14 texts (`abracadabra`, `mississippi`, `banana`, `a^30`, random DNA 110, the Fibonacci word 90, a periodic text 100, protein-like 100, Thue-Morse 80, runs 90, one letter, the empty text, surrogates, NUL / U+FFFF), 40 walks of 12 steps each per text and engine with fixed seeds (560 walks per engine, 480 for the container engines that hold byte texts only; each step one of `TryExtendRight` with a symbol or a pattern, `TryExtendLeft`, `TryParent`, `TryChild`, `TrySuffixLink`, `GetChildren`, a jump to a random locus; the argument continues the string most of the time, otherwise an absent symbol, a value that is no code unit, the terminator key -1; the walk is driven by the aliased results; a locus that is no node is given to the members that need one and both forms must refuse it; the engine that has no navigation (the Ukkonen adapter) is compared on `Count` and depth; each member must have answered and not answered at least 20 and 5 times; tallies are printed: e.g. `esa`: ExtendRightSymbol 725/725, ExtendRightPattern 549/392, ExtendLeft 547/377, Parent 328/116, Child 332/258, SuffixLink 100/219, GetChildren 200/104, 755 calls refused by both forms), and the helper `Contract/AliasProbe.cs`. "With and without the end of the text" is read as: walks that follow a suffix to its leaf, `TryChild(node, -1)` and the leaf of the empty suffix, and the texts with and without symbols above 255. No existing test changed.
- **Mutation audit (own mutants, the format of the earlier packages: `make_1b12_mutants.py`, `make_1b12_round2.py`, `run_1b12_mutants.py`, `1b12_*_listed.json`, `results_1b12_*.tsv`, `1b12_equivalent.py` in `analysis/mutation/`; a separate git worktree, Release, analyzers off, a watchdog).** Round 1, **37 mutants, 37 killed**: A01-A20 in `EsaSuffixIndex.cs` (the depth read after the write, the locus resolved after the write, the depth from the cleared result, the depth local not copied, depth by two, the depth one short, the aux wrong, a failure leaving the locus, the symbol overload's check after the write, a failure leaving the locus there, U+FFFF refused, the copy of `TryExtendLeft` taken after the write, each of the four reads of the copy replaced by the locus, the depth of the left extension one too long, a failure leaving the locus there, no code unit leaving the locus, the locus not checked), B01-B10 in `EsaSuffixIndex.Tree.cs` (the write before the read in `TryParent`, `TryChild`, `TrySuffixLink`, `GetChildren`, a re-read of the locus after the write that changes the answer only when the variable is shared (B02, B03, B05, B07, B09), an off-by-one in the size test), C01-C07 in `EsaSuffixTree.Navigation.cs` (each pass-through clears the result or the first slot first). **36 were killed by the three new classes alone, A20 (the locus check removed from `TryExtendLeft`, a mutant that has nothing to do with aliasing) by an existing class (`InvalidArguments_NeverBuildThePack`).** Round 2, written after round 1 to look for a gap, **9 mutants: 7 killed by the new classes, 2 survived stages 1 and 2 and are equivalent** (`1b12_r2_Q01`: the facade hands the index a copy of the locus; `1b12_r2_Q02`: the symbol overload hands `Extend` a copy: the fix applied one layer higher or lower, nothing observable; argued in `1b12_equivalent.py`): D01 (an empty-pattern shortcut that reads the locus after the write), D02 and D03 (a write before the call in the two overloads), E01 and E02 (a partial result on the two failure paths of the loop), L01 and L02 (the destination of `Locate` cleared first). **Total 46: 44 killed, 2 equivalent, 0 test gaps**; the control (no mutant) passes the 106. The survivors did not run the whole default set (stage 3 is optional in the runner); the two equivalents are argued, and the whole set ran on the final head without the mutants.
- **Test sets run** (policy of this file, level 2: `SuffixTree.Esa` and `SuffixTree.Core` changed, so the affected set, the categories, the checked build, EmProto and Persistent; Core only by a comment, so also `SuffixTree.Tests` and `SuffixTree.Mcp.Core.Tests`; Vectors not run: no SA / LCP kernel changed; `src/SuffixTree/Algorithms/SuffixTree.Esa` hot loops unchanged, so no gate row measured), at head `cf07f0b9` (the code is that of `08c904cd`; the commits after it are documents and audit files), one after the other, no other test or build job running (one small scratch console program was built and run for 30 seconds during the Exhaustive set, to confirm the red behaviour of the root; it is not part of the repository), Release:

- **F. Left by 1b.9d (owner decisions, none needed for S2):** (1) zero fill of the added range (Z, in the code) or the worst case reserved at the creation (W: no time, 3 (n + 1) bytes of disk in every build, 9 GB at 3x10^9); (2) a platform without `fallocate` (native macOS, Windows) and file systems that ignore it keep the free-space check and a sparse file for every output: reserve by zeros (about 1 s per GiB) or document; (3) `MappedFileStorageProvider` of `SuffixTree.Persistent` has the same window; (4) under a page-cache limit the production chain is 1.3-1.6x slower than the E7 prototype and genome-like text needs more than 0.8 of text + rank for 3x: look for the cause or keep the documented floor; (5) the figures at 3x10^9 and on a device that fills are ESTIMATES (the machine of the owner).
- **G. Left by 1b.9e (owner decisions, none needed for S2):** (1) **directory `fsync`**: the rename and the creation of the temporary file, the scratch directory and `state.json` are not forced to the directory, because the BCL cannot open a directory for `fsync` (both `File.OpenHandle` and `FileStream` throw `UnauthorizedAccessException`; tried) and only one P/Invoke (a `libc` `fsync` of a directory descriptor) would do it; the line was stopped as the brief says, the consequence is argued (a power loss costs the newest stage or the final rename, never a wrong container; `kill -9` is not affected) and untested for lack of a device: allow the P/Invoke or accept the documented consequence; (2) should `Resume` be the default of the production entry point (the cost of recording is 1.9 % of the build, a resume saves 70-94 % of a restart after a kill from the end of PH4-PH5 on and nothing measurable before it at these sizes; a failed build would keep 13 bytes per character on the disk and take a lock: a behaviour change); (3) the leaf journal of PH3 (a kill inside PH2-PH3 loses the phase: 5.5 s of 17.4 s on `dna_100m`, 14.4 s of 25.9 s on the repetitive `rep_100m`; a journal of finished leaves is exact by construction and would save up to about 43 % of a repetitive build killed late in PH3; about 250 lines and ten tests, a follow-up package); (4) a lock for builds without `Resume`, or the statement that two builds of one target without it are unsupported (they destroy each other today: the second build deletes the first one's temporary file and the first one's rename publishes the second one's half-written file); (5) **[closed by 1b.9h: a table of the 32 residues of the Singer set of 993, verified when it is used; 0.27 s per build gone from the chain, containers equal at 10^6, 10^7, 10^8]** a static cache of the difference cover (`DifferenceCover.Create` costs 0.27 s per build at X = 993: 11 % of a build of 10^7 symbols, 1.6 % of 10^8); (6) not run: macOS, Windows, arm64, network and FUSE volumes, btrfs and ZFS for the resume, a protein-like text of 10^8 and the build at 3x10^9 (hand-off package 1b.9f has the machine); the flaky 1b.3 test `NoAllocationProportionalToTheTextForTheTotal_AndOnlyTheResultForTheLengths` under load in Release (reported, not touched) **[the test part closed by 1b.9h: the counter of the thread jumps by up to 8 KB under a background collection, the test reads it in a no-GC region now]**. **(7) Added by the orchestrator's verification of 1b.9e (2026-11-22): four commits of 1b.9e lack the two trailers** (`5c8fb3a6`, `427b7f33`, `e4034910`, `867492b2`); the repair is a rewrite of about nine pushed commits and a force-push, so it is the owner's decision (recipe: `git rebase e2292b79 --exec <amend that appends both trailers>`, then `git push --force-with-lease`); the same package's later commits and all of 1b.9e.1 carry them. **(8) From 1b.9e.1:** an OS call (`fallocate` with `ZERO_RANGE` or `PUNCH_HOLE`) would make the clearing of a large unkept part a metadata operation (not needed at 10^8: +0.2-0.4 s; it could ride on the decision about the directory `fsync`), and whether the build should run the `Sections` level of `SesaVerifier` (every byte outside the payloads is zero) on what it publishes before the rename (about 0.3 s at 10^8; today it never runs the verifier). **(9) Limits of 1b.9e.1 that stay:** the cost at 3x10^9 (39 GB to read before the SA is complete) is an estimate by size and is not run; a power loss in the middle of the clearing is argued, not tested; a page of a kept range changed by another process between the CRC pass and its use is not detected.
